# 990373a Add gitkb notes for 1b93461, the PR #6 merge and the README split

## Summary
Adds gitkb knowledge notes only: 924 files under knowledge/, covering the commits fc88638 (466 files), 1b93461 (452 files), cc6e82b (3 files). It contains no code. Each commit note carries the summary, motivation and risks of one commit and links to one change note per file it touched.

## Why
The knowledge base lags git history by one commit: after new commits land, /gitkb summarizes them and the resulting notes are committed. This commit records the notes for the previous notes commit, the PR #6 merge and the README split.

## Risks
None for the code. Notes-only commits themselves need a note on the next /gitkb run, so the knowledge base is always one commit behind.
